# A/B trial measurements -- run log

> Append-only. One row per run of `evals/ab/run-trial.ts`, written by the harness, never by hand. Rows tagged
> `smoke` exercise the harness on a fixture and are **not measurements**. Tokens and cost come from the CLI's own
> `result` event (`modelUsage` summed over every model); `raw` keeps its `usage`, `modelUsage` and `subagent_stats`.
> A `usage-missing` row is invalid: the CLI reported no usage, and the trial stops for an owner ruling.
>
> **Pre-registration frozen 2026-10-31T03:12:56Z (owner-signed, SPRINT-152 T3):** `evals/ab/PREREGISTRATION.md` sha256
> `e72002cf1d1be69586833ca37133c6bcb64fc47655d51270a2dba122cd64393b`, at commit `bc1ddf4a`. Every row with a `ts` before
> that instant is tagged smoke and ran on the fixture repo (13 of 13 at the freeze); a measured trial is valid only after it.
>
> **Pre-registration re-frozen 2026-10-31T07:30:00Z (owner re-signed, SPRINT-152 T4, Amendment 1):** `evals/ab/PREREGISTRATION.md` sha256
> `83750fc4f4cc34c096da0b77c79f48e4733937be938f78678f84dcf3e2f6909e`, at commit `eab31280`; supersedes the line above. The
> amendment adds a 45-minute whole-trial deadline and a `timeout` exit label; the classification is unchanged. No trial ran
> between the two freezes: the 13 rows below are all smoke runs on the fixture repo.
>
> **Pre-registration re-frozen 2026-10-31T10:45:42Z (owner re-signed, SPRINT-152 T4, Amendment 2):** `evals/ab/PREREGISTRATION.md` sha256
> `ce811aa31e2fbf04eef0179976a30800aecd454ab56256d09236efdb124c266c`, at commit `ef9a66bc`; supersedes the line above. The
> amendment adds the per-trial environment provisioned before the clock, directory layers, one G2 revise round, owner-ruled
> voids and not-applicable graders; the four pilot rows of 07:33 to 07:39 ran under the defective harness and are voided by
> appended void rows.
>
> **Pre-registration re-frozen 2026-10-31T11:34:21Z (owner re-signed, SPRINT-152 T4, Amendment 3):** `evals/ab/PREREGISTRATION.md` sha256
> `60e7e1414fde7b192951712be3ce33f4ecf0fc9158577c0624b6c1da46aae456`, at commit `ad91c23c`; supersedes the line above. The
> amendment allows every Bash and PowerShell command for both arms and keeps build output out of the changed set; the four
> re-pilot rows of 10:51 to 11:05 ran under the old allow-list and judge input and are voided by appended void rows.
>
> **Pre-registration re-frozen 2026-10-31T12:07:44Z (owner re-signed, SPRINT-152 T4, Amendment 4):** `evals/ab/PREREGISTRATION.md` sha256
> `ac0b934e89647b7a7c48b6113adbf854cd1570026399e34825cf42eab138760e`, at commit `e1a101cb`; supersedes the line above. The
> amendment also allows the Write and Edit tools everywhere for both arms; the four second re-pilot rows of 11:39 to 11:56 ran
> before it and are voided by appended void rows. The full baseline runs after this line.

{"tool":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\Public\\workdoo-type-filter-check\\check.mjs","content":"import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';\nimport { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\nconst root = process.argv[2];\nconst { PrivateClient, privateWorkspaceHarness } = await import(pathToFileURL(`${root}/scripts/lib/private-workspace-harness.mjs`).href);\nconst harness = await privateWorkspaceHarness();\nconst log = (label, value) => console.log(`${label}: ${JSON.stringify(value)}`);\ntry {\n  await harness.start();\n  const owner = new PrivateClient(harness.origin);\n  await owner.signUp('Avery Access', 'avery@type.example');\n  const base = `/api/workspaces/${(await owner.request('POST', '/api/workspaces', { name: 'Type Filter Co' })).data.workspace.id}`;\n  const operator = new PrivateClient(harness.origin);\n  await operator.signUp('Blake Operator', 'blake@type.example');\n  const accepted = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations`, { email: 'blake@type.example' });\n  const token = new URLSearchParams(new URL(accepted.data.invitePath, harness.origin).hash.slice(1)).get('invite');\n  await operator.request('POST', '/api/invitations/accept', { token });\n  for (const email of ['r1@type.example', 'r2@type.example', 'r3@type.example']) {\n    const created = await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations`, { email });\n    assert.equal((await owner.request('POST', `${base}/invitations/${created.data.invitation.id}/revoke`, {})).status, 200);\n  }\n  const roster = (await owner.request('GET', `${base}/people`)).data.members;\n  const operatorMember = roster.find(member => member.role === 'operator');\n  const organize = body => owner.request('POST', `${base}/organization/commands`, body);\n  const team = (await organize({ commandId: randomUUID(), type: 'save_group', kind: 'team', expectedRevision: 0, name: 'Core' })).data.command.result.groupId;\n  assert.equal((await organize({ commandId: randomUUID(), type: 'set_group_member', kind: 'team', groupId: team, expectedRevision: 1, membershipId: operatorMember.id, member: true })).status, 200);\n  const get = async query => (await owner.request('GET', `${base}/access/activity${query}`));\n  const all = (await get('')).data;\n  log('unfiltered total / types', [all.total, all.events.map(e => e.type)]);\n  const revoked = (await get('?type=invitation.revoked')).data;\n  log('?type=invitation.revoked total / types', [revoked.total, revoked.events.map(e => e.type)]);\n  const paged = (await get('?type=invitation.revoked&pageSize=2&page=2')).data;\n  log('revoked pageSize=2 page=2 total / count', [paged.total, paged.events.length]);\n  const teamAdded = (await get('?type=team.member_added')).data;\n  log('?type=team.member_added total / types', [teamAdded.total, teamAdded.events.map(e => e.type)]);\n  const withMember = (await get(`?type=invitation.accepted&membershipId=${operatorMember.id}`)).data;\n  log('invitation.accepted + membershipId total', withMember.total);\n  const withMemberNone = (await get(`?type=invitation.revoked&membershipId=${operatorMember.id}`)).data;\n  log('invitation.revoked + operator membershipId total', withMemberNone.total);\n  const withSearch = (await get('?type=invitation.revoked&search=r2%40type')).data;\n  log('invitation.revoked + search=r2@type total / summaries', [withSearch.total, withSearch.events.map(e => e.summary)]);\n  for (const query of ['?type=bogus', '?type=', '?type=factory.created']) {\n    const bad = await get(query);\n    log(`${query} status / code`, [bad.status, bad.data.error?.code]);\n  }\n} finally { await harness.stop(); }\n"}} | {"tool":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\Users\\HYPEAM~1\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\sod-probe-ae41ac9b-9603-47fc-83e3-d284cc151596\\probe.mjs","content":"// Throwaway SELF_ONBOARDING probe; not part of the repo. Usage: node probe.mjs <repoRoot>\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { spawn } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport os from 'node:os';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';\nimport { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';\n\nconst root = path.resolve(process.argv[2]);\nconst temp = await mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'sod-db-'));\nconst dbPath = path.join(temp, 'workdoo.sqlite');\nconst child = spawn(process.execPath, ['server/index.ts'], { cwd: root, stdio: ['ignore','pipe','pipe'],\n  env: { ...process.env, NODE_ENV:'test', WORKDOO_PORT:'0', WORKDOO_HOST:'127.0.0.1', WORKDOO_DATABASE_PATH: dbPath,\n    WORKDOO_AUTH_SECRET:'sod-probe-secret-only-not-for-deployment-0000032', WORKDOO_APP_ORIGIN:'http://127.0.0.1:4173' } });\nconst base = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { let out='';\n  const timer = setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error(out)),20000);\n  child.stdout.on('data',c=>{ out+=c; const m=out.match(/WORKDOO_LISTENING (\\d+)/); if (m) { clearTimeout(timer); resolve(`http://127.0.0.1:${m[1]}`); } });\n  child.stderr.on('data',c=>{ out+=c; }); child.once('exit',code=>reject(new Error(`exit ${code}: ${out}`))); });\nclass Client { cookie='';\n  async request(method,url,body,extra={}) {\n    const r = await fetch(`${base}${url}`,{method,headers:{...(this.cookie?{cookie:this.cookie}:{}),\n      ...(body!==undefined?{'content-type':'application/json',origin:'http://127.0.0.1:4173','x-workdoo-request':'1'}:{}),...extra},\n      ...(body!==undefined?{body:JSON.stringify(body)}:{})});\n    const c = r.headers.getSetCookie(); if (c.length) this.cookie=c.map(x=>x.split(';')[0]).filter(x=>!x.endsWith('=')).join('; ');\n    return { status:r.status, data: await r.json() };\n  } }\nlet checks = 0;\nconst ok = (cond, label) => { assert(cond, label); checks++; console.log('PASS', label); };\ntry {\n  const ada = new Client();\n  assert.equal((await ada.request('POST','/api/auth/sign-up/email',{name:'Ada Owner',email:'ada@example.test',password:'probe-password-ada-0001'})).status,200);\n  const orgId = (await ada.request('POST','/api/workspaces',{name:'Probe Co'})).data.workspace.id;\n  const adaId = (await ada.request('GET',`/api/workspaces/${orgId}`)).data.members[0].id;\n  async function join(name,email) {\n    const inv = await ada.request('POST',`/api/workspaces/${orgId}/invitations`,{email});\n    assert.equal(inv.status,201,JSON.stringify(inv.data));\n    const c = new Client();\n    assert.equal((await c.request('POST','/api/auth/sign-up/email',{name,email,password:`probe-password-${name}-0001`})).status,200);\n    assert.equal((await c.request('POST','/api/invitations/accept',{token:inv.data.invitePath.split('#invite=')[1]})).status,200);\n    return (await ada.request('GET',`/api/workspaces/${orgId}`)).data.members.find(m=>m.email===email).id;\n  }\n  const boId = await join('Bo','bo@example.test');\n  const cyId = await join('Cy','cy@example.test');\n  const config = async responsibilities => { const cur=(await ada.request('GET',`/api/workspaces/${orgId}/onboarding/configuration`)).data.configuration;\n    const r = await ada.request('PUT',`/api/workspaces/${orgId}/onboarding/configuration`,{expectedRevision:cur.revision,enabled:true,responsibilities});\n    assert.equal(r.status,200,JSON.stringify(r.data)); };\n  const post = (body,key=randomUUID()) => ada.request('POST',`/api/workspaces/${orgId}/onboarding`,body,{'idempotency-key':key});\n  const intake = (fullName,email,managerMembershipId) => ({person:{fullName,...(email!==undefined?{email}:{})},roleTitle:'Engineer',startDate:'2026-11-02',managerMembershipId});\n  const total = async () => (await ada.request('GET',`/api/workspaces/${orgId}/onboarding`)).data.total;\n  const rejected = (r,label) => ok(r.status===422 && r.data.error?.code==='SELF_ONBOARDING', `${label} -> ${r.status} ${r.data.error?.code}`);\n\n  rejected(await post(intake('Ada Again','ADA@Example.Test',adaId)),'owner+all duties, mixed-case email');\n  await config({hr:cyId,equipment:adaId,access:adaId});\n  rejected(await post(intake('Bo Self','bo@example.test',boId)),'manager-only match (Bo holds no responsibility)');\n  rejected(await post(intake('Cy Self','Cy@EXAMPLE.test',boId)),'HR-only match (manager is Bo)');\n  await config({hr:adaId,equipment:cyId,access:adaId});\n  rejected(await post(intake('Cy Self','cy@example.test',adaId)),'equipment-only match');\n  await config({hr:adaId,equipment:adaId,access:cyId});\n  rejected(await post(intake('Cy Self','cy@example.test',adaId)),'access-only match');\n  ok(await total()===0,'nothing created after rejections (case total 0)');\n  const boKey = randomUUID();\n  const boCase = await post(intake('Bo Joiner','bo@example.test',adaId),boKey);\n  ok(boCase.status===201,`member with no duty on the case accepted -> ${boCase.status}`);\n  ok((await post(intake('No Email',undefined,cyId))).status===201,'intake with no email accepted even with a duty holder as manager');\n  ok((await post(intake('Outsider','dee@example.test',adaId))).status===201,'non-member email accepted');\n  await config({hr:boId,equipment:adaId,access:adaId});\n  const replay = await post(intake('Bo Joiner','bo@example.test',adaId),boKey);\n  ok(replay.status===200 && replay.data.case.id===boCase.data.case.id,`replay of accepted intake after Bo gained HR duty -> ${replay.status}`);\n  ok((await post(intake('Bo Second','bo2@example.test',adaId))).status===201,'other intakes still accepted');\n  ok(await total()===4,'case total 4');\n  child.kill(); await new Promise(r=>child.once('exit',r));\n  const db = new DatabaseSync(dbPath);\n  const people = db.prepare('SELECT email FROM people ORDER BY email').all().map(r=>r.email);\n  db.close();\n  ok(JSON.stringify(people)===JSON.stringify([null,'bo2@example.test','bo@example.test','dee@example.test']),`people rows: ${JSON.stringify(people)}`);\n  console.log(`SOD probe: ${checks} checks passed.`);\n} finally {\n  if (child.exitCode===null) { child.kill(); await new Promise(r=>child.once('exit',r)); }\n  await rm(temp,{recursive:true,force:true});\n}\n"}} |
